````markdown
## Part 4: Pack Generator

Agent builds a full project from the user's idea and returns ONE `.sh` pack script (`scripts/make_pack_script.py`). The user runs it in Termux (`bash <file>.sh` creates the project, builds worlds, pushes; `bash <file>.sh stage` only copies it to `MCPEDL/incoming/`) or runs GitHub workflow **Create Project from Base64** with `payload_file` = the `.sh` (the workflow only reads the payload block, it never runs the file).

Source of truth = scripts: `scripts/build_payload.py` (packager + checks), `scripts/doc_template.py`, `scripts/import_project_payload.py` from ALNStudios/MCPE-Curseforges. ALWAYS read them before building. Disagreement with this file: tell the user. Read them through Context7 `/allenscreations/mcpe-curseforges` (1.17); an attached `tools.txt` wins on any difference. Complete text not retrievable: ask the user to attach `tools.txt`, use that exact text. NEVER rewrite a script from memory. NEVER use a stale copy.

### 4.0 Ask the type first
Type unknown: ask exactly this, wait:
> What do you want to make: **addon** (.mcaddon), **map** (.mcworld), **script** (.mcpack), or **minigame** (.mcaddon with the Controller item)?

NEVER guess the type. At most ONE more question, only if the answer changes the build.

| Type | Folder | Extra |
|---|---|---|
| addon | `MCPEDL/Addons/<Name>/` | `.mcaddon` |
| map | `MCPEDL/Maps/<Name>/` | `.mcworld`; Creative, Survival, Hardcore worlds |
| script | `MCPEDL/Script/<Name>/` | `.mcpack`; BP only |
| minigame | `MCPEDL/Minigame/<Name>/` | `.mcaddon`; Controller, RP |

### 4.0a Research and Plan First (MANDATORY)
Before generating any files, scratch workspaces, or pack `.sh` scripts:
1. **Deep Research (~90% Thoroughness)**: Investigate the user's idea thoroughly (~90% confidence). Query Context7 (1.17) for Bedrock vanilla item/block/entity components, event hooks, mechanics, and limits.
2. **Select Latest Best API**: Determine and verify the latest stable `@minecraft/server` and `@minecraft/server-ui` versions and modern component `format_version`. Ensure compatibility against `min_engine_version`.
3. **Present the Plan**: Outline the implementation plan to the user:
   - Target project type and folder path.
   - Core gameplay mechanics and Bedrock systems used.
   - Modules, APIs, components, and events.
   - File manifest (manifests, scripts, functions, items/blocks, documentation).
   - Any limits, fallbacks, or trade-offs.
4. **Wait for User Approval**: Await user review and confirmation ("go" or requested changes). NEVER build files, scratch directories, or `.sh` scripts until the user explicitly approves the plan.

### 4.1 Hard rules
- NEVER write base64 or the `.sh` by hand. Create files in scratch `build/<Name>/`, then RUN `make_pack_script.py` (4.8). Cannot run code: reply `CANNOT BUILD` (4.11). NEVER invent base64.
- Deliver ONLY the one pack `.sh`. NEVER deliver a separate `.zip`, pack file (`.mcaddon`/`.mcworld`/`.mcpack`), `.json`, `.js`, `.b64` or any other file; every file lives inside the `.sh`. `build/` is scratch: never delivered, never committed.
- NEVER invent features, versions, links, assets, results, compatibility. ZIP build proves structure only.
- ALWAYS look up latest stable Bedrock + `@minecraft/server` + `@minecraft/server-ui` at time of work, record date (1.2). NEVER preview/beta/RC unless a requested feature needs it (then disclose in DESCRIPTION). `min_engine_version` per 1.2. Cannot confirm which game version first shipped your module version: say so.
- Art: vanilla item and block icons ONLY from MCicons (`reference/mcicons/mcicons-<ver>.tgz` via `scripts/lib/mcicons.py`; only `minecraft:` ids; no icon for an id = report it); NEVER Samples, Wiki or other icon sources. NEVER AI-generated images; new custom textures ONLY as pixel JSON (4.13). NEVER CurseForge/MCPEDL listing images in the payload (linked by URL from `assets/Images/`). Trader Ledger (maps) and Controller (minigames) icons ONLY from the repo copy that `build_payload.py` installs. Other textures: real vanilla names, user-supplied, pixel JSON (4.13), or omit.
- Gap in the idea: choose the most reasonable option, state it in DESCRIPTION.md.
- Shell may be plain `sh`: NEVER brace expansion. After creating files, list them, confirm they exist.
- `.sh` names: 1.1 rule (`(Repofilename)_(Version)_(Rand).sh`).
- NEVER comments in `.js`, `.mcfunction`, `.json`. Prefer event-driven logic. NEVER claim a README/catalog link to a `.mcworld` that does not exist yet.
- Structure files: Bedrock structure (.mcstructure, little-endian NBT) and raw NBT (.nbt) files are permitted ONLY under behavior_pack/structures/ (an optional namespace subfolder like structures/aln/<name>.mcstructure is fine). Generated only by code, no comments, max 2 MB per file, no spaces in paths, "not tested in game" until verified in Minecraft.

- Every reply = the three 4.11 sections (File Needed, Changelog, Termux Script). NEVER a Summary section.
- Vanilla limit blocks a requested mechanic: follow 4.12.

### 4.2 Delivery layout
ZIP root = project folder contents, built in `build/<Name>/`:
```
project.json          REQUIRED: {"type": "addon|map|script|minigame", "name": "Display Name"}
README.md  DESCRIPTION.md  CHANGELOG.md
behavior_pack/manifest.json
behavior_pack/...     functions/ items/ blocks/ entities/ recipes/ loot_tables/ texts/ structures/ scripts/main.js

                      scripts/lib/README.md (REQUIRED with main.js), lib/*.js optional
resource_pack/        map/minigame REQUIRED. addon optional. script FORBIDDEN.
texture_sources/      optional pixel JSON for custom textures (4.13). NEVER inside resource_pack/.
```
- NEVER ship `oneblock.json` (importer writes for maps), `LOCK` (importer writes for add-ons), another type's `project.json`.
- NEVER include: `db/`, `level.dat`, `world_behavior_packs.json`, `world_resource_packs.json`, `.mcworld/.mcpack/.mcaddon/.zip`, symlinks, `Mcpeworlds/...` or `assets/...` paths. NEVER name a file/folder exactly `db`.
- Limits (see `build_payload.py`): 500 files, 5 MB/file (2 MB for .mcstructure / .nbt), 25 MB total. Delivery = the one pack `.sh`, nothing else.

- NEVER create `test_world.json` or promise a test world (removed). The importer creates `assets/snapshots/`, `assets/recipes/`, `assets/items/` itself: NEVER ship `assets/`.

### 4.3 Manifests (all types)
`behavior_pack/manifest.json` and `resource_pack/manifest.json` (if present). Part 1 (1.2, 1.3) applies.
- `format_version` 2. `min_engine_version` = looked-up `[1, RELEASE, PATCH]` (game 26.60 = `[1, 26, 60]`, NEVER `[26, 60, 0]`), NEVER `[0,0,0]`.
- `header.name` = `Add-on Name (vX)`: one terminal suffix == `header.version`. Both packs same version.

- `header.description` contains `by ALNStudios` exactly once. NEVER in name.
- Fresh unique v4 UUIDs for every header and module. NEVER reuse/copy from examples.
- RP exists: BP `dependencies` include `{ "uuid": "<RP header.uuid>", "version": [x, y, z] }` matching.
- Script module: `"type": "script"`, `"language": "javascript"`, `"entry": "scripts/main.js"`; deps `{ "module_name": "@minecraft/server", "version": "<latest stable string>" }`. Add `@minecraft/server-ui` for forms (always for Controller).
- Script `lib/` folder: EVERY pack that has a `behavior_pack/scripts/main.js` ALSO has `behavior_pack/scripts/lib/README.md` (create the folder together with `main.js`, never later). The README is for the AI review: what the pack's script code does, one line per file in `scripts/` and `scripts/lib/` (purpose, what it exports or registers, what it depends on), the script events and custom item/entity ids it uses, and known limits. When `lib/` holds no `.js` files yet, the README says so in one line. Never write library IDs, query text or the word Context7 in it (2.2). Update it whenever the scripts change.
- Helper code in `lib/` (only when `main.js` grows past one readable file, e.g. shared helpers, constants, form builders): put the helpers in `behavior_pack/scripts/lib/<name>.js` and import them from `main.js` with a relative path and the `.js` extension (`import "./lib/<name>.js";`). `entry` stays `scripts/main.js`. Verified form (Context7 2026-11-21): relative `./file.js` import from the entry file; named exports from `lib/` files are not verified, query before using them. A small pack keeps everything in `main.js` and only the README in `lib/`.
- NEVER experiments unless needed (name in docs). Deps/imports ONLY `@minecraft/server`, `@minecraft/server-ui`, `@minecraft/vanilla-data`.
- RP template (replace every placeholder; run `python3 -m json.tool`):
```json
{
  "format_version": 2,
  "header": {
    "name": "Add-on Name (v1)",
    "description": "Textures for the add-on. by ALNStudios",
    "uuid": "<fresh uuid v4>",
    "version": [1, 0, 0],
    "min_engine_version": [1, LOOKED_UP_RELEASE, LOOKED_UP_PATCH]
  },
  "modules": [
    { "type": "resources", "uuid": "<fresh uuid v4>", "version": [1, 0, 0] }
  ],
  "metadata": { "authors": ["ALNStudios"], "product_type": "addon" }
}
```

### 4.4 Type: addon
- `.mcaddon`. BP required, RP optional. No void cleaner, ledger, Controller.
- `functions/tick.json` names MUST exist as `functions/<name>.mcfunction`.
- NEVER create `LOCK`.

### 4.5 Type: map (OneBlock)
Works in any database-free void world. NEVER depend on terrain, database, world settings.
Required (`build_payload.py` installs the 3 ledger files if missing):
```
behavior_pack/functions/tick.json              {"values": ["void_cleaner", "oneblock_tick"]}  void_cleaner FIRST
behavior_pack/functions/void_cleaner.mcfunction
behavior_pack/functions/oneblock_setup.mcfunction
behavior_pack/items/trader_ledger.json
resource_pack/textures/item_texture.json
resource_pack/textures/items/trader_ledger.png
```
1. `tick.json` valid, non-empty `values`, `void_cleaner` first, every name exists.
2. `void_cleaner.mcfunction` FIRST command, own line, no trailing spaces:
   ```
   execute as @a[tag=!void_cleaned] run fill -16 -64 -16 16 -64 16 air
   ```
3. `oneblock_setup.mcfunction`: exists, harmless, idempotent. NEVER in `tick.json`. DESCRIPTION says in plain words (no file name) that the optional setup command needs cheats and cheats disable achievements.
4. Trader Ledger MANDATORY, ONLY from repo copy (32x32 RGBA `Mcpeworlds/assets/resources_pack/textures/items/trader_ledger.png`). Identifier stays `allenscreations:trader_ledger`; display name may change. NEVER redraw/recolor/resize/re-encode. NEVER copy the whole `Mcpeworlds/assets/`.
5. Ledger role follows the idea (Dirtbound: only way to open a shop menu). No role: still ship, say so in DESCRIPTION.md.
6. Progression data pool: Every OneBlock map's Script module uses the centralized data pool at `behavior_pack/scripts/lib/Oneblock_Progression.js` (copied from `Mcpeworlds/Progression/Oneblock_Progression.js`). The file contains only the blocks, loot, and mob pools; map-specific spawn chances, island rules, and event triggers remain in the map's own scripts. The importer installs it automatically if omitted.

Pipeline (Dirtbound design) unless user says otherwise. `void_cleaner.mcfunction` runs every tick, each command guarded `tag=!void_cleaned`, order: (1) required Y=-64 line; (2) teleport untagged player to spawn (only function-side teleport); (3) Bedrock under core; (4) placeholder at core only if empty; (5) world spawn; (6) optional notice; (7) add `void_cleaned` tag LAST, CONDITIONAL on core Bedrock existing (`execute as @a[tag=!void_cleaned] if block 0 -1 0 bedrock run tag @s add void_cleaned`). Without the tag every guarded command repeats forever; unconditional tag never retries a fill on unloaded chunks. NEVER add a "clear starting area" step.
- ALWAYS skip any core-area clear once the core exists.
- `oneblock_tick`: restores only missing Bedrock, only if a player has `void_cleaned`. NEVER reset spawn, teleport, place/replace core.
- Script API owning the core waits for `void_cleaned` before replacing the placeholder.
- Standard map coords (EVERY map, ALWAYS): OneBlock core `(0,64,0)`, Bedrock under core `(0,63,0)`, spawn `(0,65,0)`. NEVER deviate unless the user explicitly overrides. Every `fill` MUST cover what it claims to clear. `setworldspawn 0 65 0` and `spawnpoint @a 0 65 0` in `void_cleaner.mcfunction`; core placeholder/block placed at `0 64 0`; Bedrock guard checks `if block 0 63 0 bedrock`.
- Script teleport only in script, state it in summary. Idea cannot fit the `void_cleaner`-first pipeline: say so, NEVER silently change it.

### 4.6 Types: script and minigame
Script: standalone BP for general logic. `project.json` type `script`, `behavior_pack/manifest.json`, `behavior_pack/scripts/main.js`. NEVER RP or Controller. Always `.mcpack`.

Minigame: playable challenge driven by a script. `project.json` type `minigame`, BP + RP. Always `.mcaddon`. Uses the Controller.

Required (`build_payload.py` installs Controller files if missing):
```
behavior_pack/manifest.json                    script module + @minecraft/server + @minecraft/server-ui
behavior_pack/scripts/main.js                  the game only; imports ./controller.js
behavior_pack/scripts/controller.js            fixed copy installed by build_payload.py, NEVER edited
behavior_pack/items/controller.json
behavior_pack/functions/give_controller.mcfunction
behavior_pack/functions/end_game.mcfunction
resource_pack/manifest.json
resource_pack/textures/item_texture.json
resource_pack/textures/items/controller.png    16 x 16, from repo, NEVER redrawn
```
Controller (item `aln:<minigame_name>_controller`; NEVER opt out): ALN UI Redesign main menu per `INSTRUCTIONS.md` 1.13/1.14 with exactly 3 buttons **Start**, **About**, **Exit** (this order); while running the first button = **End Game**. All of it lives in `controller.js`: NEVER build another form.
- Only host/operator gets/uses it, via `isHost` in `controller.js`, NEVER `player.isOp()` alone. Start works while not running (re-read state after form closes). Running: End Game -> confirm form (**End Game** / **Back**) -> `endGame()`, no cheats. Start opens How to play (rules, **Start Game**, **Back**). About: creator, pack + version, one line, profiles, Back.
- KEPT while the game runs. `consumeOnStart: true` restores old behavior (Start consumes first Controller unless `getGameMode()` is `GameMode.Creative`; End Game then only reachable by a host still carrying one).
- Game logic calls `endGame()` on win/lose. `endGame()` sets `ended`, gives host/operator a fresh Controller if none.
- Stuck state: NEVER `running` after reload with no live game. Reset to `idle` on world load, give Controller (1.11 item 4). `give_controller`/`end_game` need cheats: last resort.
- Giving: host/operator on every spawn incl. death when carrying none (`consumeOnStart: true`: first spawn when not `running`; Creative operators always). State `idle|running|ended` in a world dynamic property.
- Form shows state and whether Controller is kept. NEVER add Restart/other buttons. Fill `about`, `gameInfo` and `howToPlay` with real text and NO `§` color codes. Colors, the ALNStudios title, bold text, Pixelify font and action bar feedback are done by `controller.js`. The importer copies the skin font + About icon from `assets/aln-ui-redesign/` into the RP and `lib/pixelify.js` is installed with `controller.js`: NEVER ship or edit them.
- `controller.js` installed when `main.js` calls `registerController(`; check fails if it differs, NEVER edit. Write BP manifest with looked-up `min_engine_version` BEFORE running the script (item JSON takes `format_version` from it). You write `resource_pack/manifest.json` (4.3, fresh UUIDs, same version) and add its dependency + `@minecraft/server-ui` to the BP manifest. NEVER copy anything else from `Mcpeworlds/assets/`.
`main.js` template:
```js
import { world } from "@minecraft/server";
import { registerController, endGame } from "./controller.js";
function win() {
  world.sendMessage("You won.");
  endGame();
}
registerController({
  title: "Sample Game",
  version: "1.0.0",
  about: "One plain sentence about the pack.",
  gameInfo: "Type: Challenge\nPlayers: 1 or more\nGoal: Replace this text with a short description of the game.",
  howToPlay: "Goal: replace this text.\nControls: replace this text.\nHow it ends: replace this text.",
  onStart: () => {
    world.sendMessage("Game started.");
  },
  onEnd: () => {
    world.sendMessage("Game ended.");
  },
});
```
- `title`, `version` (EXACTLY the Behavior Pack manifest version, bump both together), `about`, `gameInfo`, `howToPlay` required; `consumeOnStart` optional (default false). `onStart(player)` throws: state -> `idle`, Controller returned. `onEnd()` runs via `endGame()`. Script event `allenscreations:end` also ends the game. Template `win()` only sends a message: REPLACE with real logic. Old inline Controllers pass with a warning.

### 4.7 Recipes
- Every custom shaped/shapeless crafting recipe: unlock per 1.4. Other recipe types: no `unlock`.

### 4.8 Build the pack script
NEVER hand-write ZIP, base64 or the `.sh`. `scripts/make_pack_script.py` installs missing Ledger/Controller files, runs every `build_payload.py` check, builds the ZIP in memory and writes ONE Termux `.sh` that carries the project as a base64 block at its end. It writes nothing else. The importer reads that same block.
1. Create `build/<Name>/` per 4.2 incl. `project.json` + BP manifest with looked-up `min_engine_version`. `build/` is scratch.
2. Get `scripts/make_pack_script.py`, `build_payload.py`, `import_project_payload.py`, `doc_template.py`, plus `pixel_json_to_png.py` when `texture_sources/` exists (complete text from `/allenscreations/mcpe-curseforges` via Context7, or from the attached `tools.txt`), all in one folder next to `build/`.
3. With `texture_sources/`: `python3 pixel_json_to_png.py build/Name` first, fix every FAIL line, rerun (4.13). Then `python3 make_pack_script.py build/Name` (`--check-only` to test only).
4. Fix every failure incl. every `DOC:`, rerun. Put the SELF-TEST PASSED + PACK SCRIPT OK + BUILD OK (+ PIXEL TEXTURES OK when textures were drawn) result after the file name in File Needed. Proves structure only. Node MUST be installed for syntax check; if output says not checked, say so.
5. Run `python3 doc_template.py --style build/Name`; rewrite every STYLE hint with a specific fact, rebuild.
6. Deliver ONLY `<Name>_<Version>_<Rand>.sh` (fresh Rand, 1.1 rule). NEVER retype or edit it.

### 4.9 Final self-check before replying
- [ ] Type asked/given before building.
- [ ] `behavior_pack/scripts/lib/README.md` exists whenever `main.js` does.
- [ ] Context7 queried (1.17) before code, manifests, JSON and version claims; four fixed libraries only; `Docs queried` bullet written.
- [ ] SELF-TEST PASSED + PACK SCRIPT OK + BUILD OK printed; exactly one `.sh` delivered, no other file.
- [ ] Module versions latest stable, date recorded. `min_engine_version` looked up in the form `[1, RELEASE, PATCH]`, docs state only a verified minimum.
- [ ] Docs state achievement status (+cheats caveat), experiments, "not tested in game"; no `DOC:` failures; no STYLE hints; tagline one concrete sentence.
- [ ] Every README/DESCRIPTION/CHANGELOG matches `DOCS_RULES.md` exactly, no placeholders, DESCRIPTION has both listing images.
- [ ] Map: `oneblock_setup` harmless+idempotent; `void_cleaner` ends with conditional `void_cleaned` tag.
- [ ] Script: BP only, `.mcpack`, no Controller/RP, real `main.js` logic.
- [ ] Minigame: Controller unchanged; `title`, `version` == BP manifest, `about`, real `gameInfo`/`howToPlay` without color codes; real logic calling `endGame()`; stuck-state recovery.
- [ ] No comments in scripts/functions/JSON. Versions `vX` format only.
- [ ] No `Mcpeworlds/assets/` file copied except ledger/controller; nothing from `assets/Images/`.
- [ ] Any `.sh` named `(Repofilename)_(Version)_(Rand).sh`, fresh Rand >=5.
- [ ] Reply = only the three 4.11 sections (or one exception).

### 4.10 Build failures
Paste the workflow error log back to the agent. Find the rule here and the check in `build_payload.py`/`doc_template.py`.
| Message contains | Fix |
|---|---|
| `type must be one of` | `project.json` type: addon, map, script, minigame. |
| `File type not allowed` / `world data not allowed` / `link files not allowed` | Remove it (4.2). |
| `header.name must end with one (vX)` / `title version does not match` | One suffix == `header.version`, both packs. |

| `description needs 'by ALNStudios' exactly once` | Once per description, never in name. |
| `must depend on the resource pack header.uuid` | Add dependency (4.3). |
| `first entry must be void_cleaner` / `lacks the exact required fill line` / `the last command must add the void_cleaned tag` | Fix `tick.json`, `void_cleaner.mcfunction` (4.5). |
| `missing required file` | Add the file for your type. |
| `is not a byte-for-byte copy` | Use the repo PNG unchanged. |
| `controller.js must be the unchanged generator copy` | Delete it, rerun the script. |
| `registerController needs ...` | Fill the call's fields (`version` must equal the BP manifest version). |
| `import ... is not allowed` / `eval and the Function constructor` / `syntax error` | Fix the named line. |
| `min_engine_version must be 3 integers` | Use looked-up game version. |
| Minecraft import: `min_engine_version` too high ("highest value we accept") | Use `[1, RELEASE, PATCH]` (game 26.60 = `[1, 26, 60]`), at most the highest released version. |
| `format_version ... newer than min_engine_version` | Raise `min_engine_version` or lower item `format_version`. |
| `is already used by MCPEDL/...` | Fresh UUIDs. |
| `Name clash` / `already exists` / `also exists under` | Other folder name or `overwrite` true. |
| `test worlds were removed` | Delete `test_world.json` from the project (no test world exists anymore). |
| `missing the unlock context` | Add unlock (1.4). |
| `clickable CurseForge image` / `clickable MCPEDL image` | Paste the exact HTML line from `DOCS_RULES.md` 2.5 into Creator and Permissions. |
| `run scripts/optimize_images.py` | Compress `assets/Images/` (workflow does it). |
| `old hardcoded-version Minecraft Bedrock badge` | Use the version-free badge from `DOCS_RULES.md` 2.5. |
| `DOC: ...` | Fix exactly what it names; template in `DOCS_RULES.md` 2.5. |

### 4.11 Reply format
Every reply = exactly these three sections, this order, these headings (`INSTRUCTIONS.md` Reply Format). NOTHING before, between or after. NEVER a Summary section. Empty: `- None`.

**File Needed:** the one pack `.sh`, attached as the downloadable file, then its check result on the same line, e.g. `<Name>_<Version>_<Rand>.sh (SELF-TEST PASSED, PACK SCRIPT OK, BUILD OK)`. No other file.

**Changelog:** brief bullets: what the project does; `min_engine_version` and module versions with the check date (verified or not verified); `Docs queried (date): <library IDs and topics>`; deviations from this file or `None`; `Source created, not tested in game.`

**Termux Script:** ONE fenced `bash` block with ONLY the run commands, nothing else inside it:
```bash
bash ~/storage/downloads/<file>.sh
bash ~/storage/downloads/<file>.sh stage
```
First line creates the project and pushes. Second line only stages it for GitHub workflow **Create Project from Base64** (`payload_file` = `<file>.sh`). Use one or both lines as the user needs.

Exceptions (INSTEAD of the three sections, nothing else):
- Cannot run code: `CANNOT BUILD` and one line why. No files, no base64.
- Build failed, unfixable: `BUILD FAILED`, exact failing lines from `make_pack_script.py`, the rule each names. No `.sh`.
- Type unknown: only the 4.0 question.
- Planning phase (4.0a): present the ~90% research plan and await user approval before proceeding to build.

### 4.12 Vanilla Limits and Workarounds
Same rules as `INSTRUCTIONS.md` 1.12. Read it when a mechanic hits a vanilla limit.
### 4.13 Pixel textures (custom item and block art)
- The ONLY way to draw new art: pixel JSON, rendered to PNG by `scripts/pixel_json_to_png.py`. NEVER AI-generated images, NEVER copied or guessed art. A vanilla look = use the real vanilla texture name instead. NEVER for `script` projects (no resource_pack).
- Source: `texture_sources/<any>.json` = array of textures. NEVER inside `resource_pack/`. No comments.
- One texture: `{"name": "shepherds_pie", "size": 64, "palette": {"0": "#00000000", "1": "#3d2817"}, "pixels": [[0, 0, 1, ...], ...]}`
- `name`: lowercase letters, digits, `_`. It is the PNG name, the key in `resource_pack/textures/item_texture.json` and the item's `minecraft:icon`. ALWAYS register it there.
- `size`: 64 standard for richer detail and styling freedom (16, 32 or 128 allowed when requested). `pixels` = exactly `size` rows of exactly `size` palette indexes.
- `palette`: keys are numbers in quotes, `"0"` = `#00000000` (transparent). Values `#RRGGBB` or `#RRGGBBAA`. Every index used MUST exist in the palette. Max 12 colors.
- Optional `folder`: `items` (default), `blocks` or `items/<sub>`. The PNG goes to `resource_pack/textures/<folder>/<name>.png`.
- Style: Minecraft pixel art, clean outlines, depth, light from the top left, no gradients, no blurry anti-aliasing, styled for 64x64 high detail. Recolors share one grid with a different palette.
- RUN `python3 pixel_json_to_png.py build/<Name>` BEFORE `make_pack_script.py` (4.8): it checks every texture, lists every problem together and writes the PNGs. Fix, rerun. NEVER hand-write a PNG or base64.
- Ship the JSON AND the PNGs. The JSON is the source: later changes edit the JSON and rerun the script, NEVER the PNG. `--preview [file]` writes a review sheet outside the project: NEVER ship it.

### 4.14 Custom Sounds
- Audio files cannot travel inside a spec or pack script. A project that needs custom sounds keeps a pure-Python synthesizer at `<project>/sound_sources/make_sounds.py` (reference: `MCPEDL/Addons/ALNSpiritSphere/sound_sources/make_sounds.py`). It writes `.ogg` files into `<project>/resource_pack/sounds/` and needs ffmpeg (Termux: `pkg install ffmpeg`).
- An update that adds sounds uses `@@ create` for `make_sounds.py` and `resource_pack/sounds/sound_definitions.json` (`format_version` 1.14.0, one event per sound, sound paths without extension, category neutral). After the update script runs, the user runs: `python3 <project>/sound_sources/make_sounds.py`, then `git add`, `commit`, `push`.
- Sound event ids use the pattern `aln.<project>.<element>.<kind>` and are played from scripts with `playSound`. NEVER guess vanilla sound ids; use only ids verified through Context7.
- New sound files need a full Minecraft client restart to load. Say so in the handoff.
- `.ogg` is the only audio type allowed. `DESCRIPTION.md` must describe sounds in player terms and never name the script, ffmpeg or file names.
````

## Policy Doc: DOCS_RULES.md

````markdown
# Docs Rules: README, DESCRIPTION, CHANGELOG, Audit and References (Parts 2-3, v3)

Single source for every doc rule and template (2.1 to 2.5), the `/checkrm` audit (3.1, 3.2) and the Minecraft Wiki and Bedrock Samples references (3.3, 3.4). `PACK_GENERATOR_INSTRUCTIONS.md` uses it too. `INSTRUCTIONS.md` Part 1 wins on conflict. `scripts/doc_template.py` enforces the templates below (`DOC:` failures). Script and file disagree: report it, follow the script for what the build accepts.

### 2.1 Roles
- README.md: version-free download landing page. Title, package format, direct link, 3 install steps, link to DESCRIPTION. NEVER gameplay text, badges, images, release version.
- Root `README.md`: public introduction only (what ALNStudios makes, where to download, install steps, links to the catalogs, attribution). NEVER a pack list, tables, badges, images, versions. The build NEVER edits it.
- DESCRIPTION.md: full product page. NEVER release version in title.
- CHANGELOG.md: player-visible changes only. Plain text, only source of truth.
- Catalogs (download source of truth, tables allowed): `MCPEDL/Addons/README.md#available-add-ons`, `MCPEDL/Maps/README.md#available-maps`, `MCPEDL/Script/README.md#available-scripts`, `MCPEDL/Minigame/README.md#available-minigames`, `builds/README.md#available-pack-and-map-builds`.
- Index: `MCPEDL/README.md` is generated by `scripts/build_mcpedl_readme.py` (run by Build Changed Packs & Sync Catalogs after the AI review files): per pack Version, Source, Package and AI review links, grouped by type. NEVER hand-edit.
- ALWAYS move verified player-facing text from a long README into DESCRIPTION before replacing it. NEVER lose verified info. NEVER duplicate full descriptions.

### 2.2 Writing
- ALWAYS player-facing: what players do, how it behaves in game, what they need.
- ALWAYS in-game display names ("Diamond"). NEVER identifiers (`minecraft:diamond`), file/function/event/API/module names, JSON, config keys, tick intervals, code, hashes, build details.
- NEVER write `Script API` or `@minecraft/server`. Use "modern Minecraft Bedrock mechanics".
- NEVER BP/RP. Write "Behavior Pack", "Resource Pack", "Complete Pack (.mcaddon)".
- NEVER code blocks or edit-JSON/JS instructions. Config = in-game options, commands, tags.
- Tone: factual, commercial, and minimal. NEVER hype, clickbait, rhetorical questions, filler, emojis, or walls of text. Keep every section short, scannable, and focused on what matters to the player.
- ALWAYS verify exact values, recipes, requirements from source or tests.
- NEVER claim "zero lag", universal multiplayer/Realms, achievement preservation, "no experimental features" without evidence for that exact claim. Exception: the Achievements line of EVERY project = `Yes, achievement-friendly.` (keep cheats/world caveats as a following sentence).
- ALWAYS say cheats are needed when documenting a cheat-only function, and that cheats disable achievements in that world.
- Page quality: tagline = one concrete sentence (<=25 words, one verified number or item name) that doubles as CurseForge Summary. NEVER "adds items to the game". Minimize text everywhere: explain a mechanic with one short example, one idea per feature card, and keep `How to Use` crystal-clear so any player immediately understands how to play. Steps = numbered. Style check: `python3 scripts/doc_template.py --style <folder>`; rewrite every STYLE hint with a fact (advisory).

### 2.3 Layout
- Must render on GitHub, CurseForge, MCPEDL, Planet Minecraft.
- NEVER hand-written Markdown tables in README/DESCRIPTION (catalogs and generated CurseForge views may use tables). Use `### Feature Name` cards with `- **What Happens**: ...`.
- Paragraphs <=4 sentences. NEVER LaTeX. Use `->`.
- README: NEVER badges/images. DESCRIPTION: exactly the 2 template badges (Minecraft Bedrock "Stable Release", never a version) plus the 2 profile banners. Other images: item sprites (`assets/items/`), recipe diagrams (`assets/recipes/`), snapshots (`assets/snapshots/`) only.
- Profile banners (distinct from the per-pack listing images in 1.5): HTML, `width="45%"`, NEVER `height`, full raw URL `https://raw.githubusercontent.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/main/assets/Images/<file>` (`curseforge.jpg`, `MCPEDL.jpg`). NEVER edit/copy them. `scripts/optimize_images.py` compresses (800 px, 200 KB, JPEG).
- NEVER duplicate version suffixes `(v1.2.0) (v1.2.1)`.
- NEVER copy a legacy MCPEDL description to CurseForge unchanged.
- Repo links: relative. Published pages: full URLs `https://github.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges/tree/main/<path>`, `.../raw/main/builds/<file>`.
- Spoiler (CurseForge `</>`): `<h2>Title</h2><div class="spoiler">content</div>`. Preview first. HTML only for listing images and a verified spoiler.
- Changelog views: `changelog_views/<Name>.github.md` + `.curseforge.html` generated by `scripts/build_changelog_views.py`. NEVER add HTML/folds/links to CHANGELOG.md. NEVER hand-edit views.
- Description HTML: rendered from DESCRIPTION.md by `scripts/lib/build_description_views.py` (a library, writes no file) into the Listing Kit below; NEVER recreate separate `Package/<Name>.curseforge.html` or `image_prompts/` files (one dark green accent (#15803d) on every page, minimal theme-following style only; hero with badges: lowest Minecraft version read from the manifests as `1.26+`, `Experimental: No` always, achievements; feature cards; Get Started steps; Commands box; NO changelog box and NO pack version; CurseForge-supported HTML only). Assets in `assets/items/`, `assets/recipes/`, and `assets/snapshots/` automatically build responsive galleries (`Custom Items`, `Crafting Recipes`, `Screenshots`); NEVER manually embed raw `<img>` tags for asset files into `DESCRIPTION.md`, as find_media skips files already present in the markdown. DESCRIPTION.md stays the source. NEVER hand-edit views.
- Listing Kit: `Package/<Name>.listing.md` generated from DESCRIPTION.md by `scripts/build_listing_kits.py` (refreshed by Build Changed Packs & Sync Catalogs). One page with Package name, Summary (max 256, the DESCRIPTION tagline as plain text), the description HTML and the Logo/Banner AI image prompts, each in its own code block so GitHub shows a Copy button. AI image generation prompts for banners/logos are generated automatically here; recipes and sprites use pixel/vector generators, NEVER AI generation. The HTML has no pack logo. NEVER hand-edit.
- Media: payloads contain no screenshots. Add them on the platform after publishing.

### 2.4 Attribution and keywords
- Creator links + profile banners: DESCRIPTION only, in Creator and Permissions. NEVER in README, changelog, manifest description.
- Attribution block (no links) and Keywords line: exact text in the DESCRIPTION template (2.5), attribution directly before Keywords.
- Keywords: end of every DESCRIPTION, from real features. Anchors: `Minecraft Bedrock add-on`, `Minecraft PE mod`, `MCPEDL add-on`, `CurseForge Bedrock`, `ALNStudios`. Gameplay anchors only if verified.

### 2.5 Templates (STRICT)
- ALWAYS follow exactly: same headings, text, order, labels, bullet format. NEVER add/remove/rename/merge/reorder sections. NEVER boilerplate or text outside the template.
- Only optional sections, in this order directly after How to Use: `## Commands` (lines `- `/command options`: what it does`; only for packs with real player commands) then `## Common Questions` (engaging hook Q&A connected to the pack's best highlights, features, or player benefits; lines `- **Question**: answer`). Omit a whole section without verified content. The CurseForge view shows Commands in its own box with a cheats note, and renders Common Questions as minimal collapsible spoilers.
- ALWAYS replace every `[placeholder]` and `verified-...` marker. NEVER leave one.
- `.mcworld` installs may vary; multiple verified world variants may be listed. 

README.md (exactly 11 non-empty lines). Direct package URL if it exists, else official category catalog. Format = real (`.mcaddon`/`.mcpack`/`.mcworld`). `.mcworld` step 3 = `3. Find the imported world in your Worlds list and open it.` Preserve all verified world links.

## Part 3: Docs Audit and References (checkrm)

### 3.1 Authority and Scope
- `INSTRUCTIONS.md` Part 1 and Part 2 here are authoritative. Unreadable: stop, report. NEVER work from memory.
- Docs and catalog maintenance ONLY. NEVER change gameplay code, manifests, UUIDs, versions, deps, pack settings, assets, workflows, release config unless explicitly asked. "Documentation-only" = touch none of those.
- Modes: repo-wide (`/checkrm`, "all projects", no single project named) | single project (name/slug/folder, or create/repair/update one project's docs).
- ALWAYS discover real folder structure first. NEVER assume a path or substitute a similar project. Report ambiguity/missing target.

### 3.2 Procedure
1. Discover: target project, README, DESCRIPTION, manifests, scripts, config, entities, items, blocks, recipes, functions, loot tables, lang files, assets; parent catalog, builds README.
2. Audit by reading (agents do not run `scripts/check_readme_status.py`; the `.sh` runs it). It checks: missing/incomplete docs, add-on READMEs with product info, short map/script READMEs, broken relative links, orphan build dirs. Cover the same ground by hand and report.
3. Verify facts BEFORE writing: what it does, features, activation/config, persistence, packs/deps, versions, limits, achievements/experiments (1.2). NEVER infer a feature from a name. Separate implemented from planned. Verify vanilla names/sprites against 3.3-3.4.
4. References: before creating/rewriting README or DESCRIPTION read category catalog, builds README, 2-3 well-documented same-category projects. Reuse conventions, NEVER project-specific claims. Verify their links/images/claims first.
5. Write per `DOCS_RULES.md`. NEVER add an unimplemented feature because requested. Omit install steps if asked.
6. Images/badges/links: README none. DESCRIPTION 2 template badges + profile banners, no other badges. Other images: item sprites, recipe diagrams, snapshots from the pack's `assets/` folders (see 1.5). Confirm assets exist, absolute raw URLs, verify link targets. No placeholders.
7. Sync catalogs and builds: check category catalog, builds README. Update only relevant entries. Search first (no duplicates). Keep ordering. Inspect build scripts before running. The `.sh` or CI runs `scripts/auto_bump_and_build.py --project "<Project-Name>"` (never changes manifest versions). NEVER claim build/sync done if failed or not run.
8. Verify: re-read whole edited file (headings, lists, links); each feature has source evidence, no verified content lost; the `.sh` runs `scripts/validate_manifest_titles.py --project <path>` and `scripts/check_readme_status.py` (expect 0 needing update, `[LINK INTEGRITY] All relative links valid`); LOCK marker for `MCPEDL/Addons/`; review diff for scope. State any check not run.
9. Report under **Changelog** (`INSTRUCTIONS.md` Reply Format): projects audited/updated, files changed, commands + results, missing assets/links/unverifiable info, remaining work. NEVER report success for an unrun or failed check.
- Bulk remake (run by the `.sh` or `remake-docs.yml`, never by the agent): `python3 scripts/check_readme_status.py --remake standardize --all --check` previews; without `--check` writes. `standardize` keeps text, refreshes READMEs as version-free pages, strips versions from DESCRIPTION titles, fixes structure, adds missing CHANGELOG entry. `--remake rebuild --project <Name>` regenerates generic DESCRIPTION/README from source (`--all` needs `--confirm-rebuild`). Defaults (Verification, Achievements, Experimental features) are placeholders: replace with verified facts. `remake-docs.yml` = dry run + PR.

### 3.3 Minecraft Wiki Item Reference
- https://minecraft.wiki/w/Item = official names, grouping, sprite look. ALWAYS together with Samples, NEVER instead.
- Ids, textures, recipes, components: Samples. Display names: Wiki Bedrock name, confirmed against Samples `resource_pack/texts/en_US.lang` (`item.<id>.name`, `tile.<id>.name`). Lang file = in-game truth if Wiki unreachable/disagrees; report disagreement.
- NEVER title-case an identifier (`experience_bottle` = Bottle o' Enchanting, `hay_block` = Hay Bale, `ender_eye` = Eye of Ender, `wooden_door` = Oak Door, `golden_rail` = Powered Rail, `magma` = Magma Block).
- Edition tags `[BE only]`/`[JE only]`. NEVER document a Java-only item as available (Spectral Arrow is not Bedrock).

### 3.4 Mojang Bedrock Samples Reference
- Wiki sprite names: `Invicon_<Title_Case_Name>.png`, `ItemSprite_<lowercase-hyphenated-name>.png`. Look up by Wiki name, NEVER identifier. Fetch with available web tool; shell blocked: report, NEVER route around, fall back to Samples. Image license rules: 1.5.
- Wiki, `en_US.lang`, Samples lag each other. ALWAYS check Samples `version.json`, state the version a claim was verified against.
- Samples = truth for vanilla ids, textures, recipes, components, entities, formats (1.1 source priority). Root `BEDROCK_SAMPLES_ROOT` (workflow clones to `/tmp/bedrock-samples`). Agents read it through Context7 `/mojang/bedrock-samples` (INSTRUCTIONS.md 1.16).
- Layout: `resource_pack/` (`textures/items/`, `textures/blocks/`, `textures/models/armor/`, `textures/ui/`, `attachables/`, `texts/`), `behavior_pack/` (`items/`, `entities/`, `recipes/`, `loot_tables/`).
- Content changes per release: ALWAYS verify checked-out version, confirm each path exists. NEVER hardcode values (armor defense, durability) from memory.
- Ids != texture filenames. Inventory all recipes, map every ingredient, report unresolved together. NEVER placeholder.
- `textures/item_texture.json`, `terrain_texture.json` start with a `//` comment line: strip before parsing. Many vanilla items have no id-keyed entry; many block textures are `.tga`: check both extensions before calling missing.
- Block icons render in game from top+side textures: build isometric view from real faces for cube sprites.
- An id valid elsewhere may not exist in Bedrock (`oak_door` = `wooden_door`). Confirm id appears in Samples recipes/loot tables/trades before documenting.
- Custom items use the add-on's own RP textures. Vanilla `behavior_pack/recipes/` = reference for recipe format, group tags, ingredient ids.
- Context7 first (1.16). Library empty or asset absent: report. Only GitHub reachable: `git clone --filter=blob:none --sparse` then `git sparse-checkout set --no-cone <paths>`.
- Scale 16x16 textures with nearest-neighbor (`Image.Resampling.NEAREST`).
````

```
// Shared constants: ids, saved-data keys, island layout and the lucky block type table with each type's chance.
export const LUCKY = "allenscreations:lucky_block";
export const TIP_KEY = "lucky_tip_shown";
export const BUILT_KEY = "island_built";
export const TUTORIAL_KEY = "tutorial_left";
export const SETUP_TAG = "void_cleaned";
export const CORE = { x: 0, y: 64, z: 0 };
export const SPAWN_BLOCK = { x: 0, y: 65, z: 0 };
export const SPAWN_STAND = { x: 0.5, y: 65, z: 0.5 };
export const CHEST_AT = { x: -1, y: 65, z: -1 };
export const STARTER = [
  ["minecraft:oak_sapling", 3],
  ["minecraft:bone_meal", 4],
  ["minecraft:bread", 4],
  ["minecraft:bucket", 1],
];

export const TYPES = {
  "allenscreations:lucky_block": { theme: "", label: "§eAll-Round", weight: 17 },
  "allenscreations:lucky_farming": { theme: "F", label: "§aFarming", weight: 14 },
  "allenscreations:lucky_building": { theme: "B", label: "§6Building", weight: 20 },
  "allenscreations:lucky_combat": { theme: "C", label: "§cCombat", weight: 14 },
  "allenscreations:lucky_mining": { theme: "M", label: "§bMining", weight: 14 },
  "allenscreations:lucky_mystic": { theme: "Y", label: "§dMystic", weight: 12 },
  "allenscreations:lucky_treasure": { theme: "T", label: "§3Treasure", weight: 9 },
};

const TOTAL_WEIGHT = Object.values(TYPES).reduce((sum, type) => sum + type.weight, 0);
for (const type of Object.values(TYPES)) type.chance = Math.round((type.weight * 100) / TOTAL_WEIGHT);
```

## FILE: MCPEDL/Maps/One-Block-Skyblock-Lucky-Blocks/behavior_pack/scripts/lib/events.js (161 lines, 5456 bytes)

```
// Physical prizes: thunder mobs, falling anvil, falling loot chest, baby shower, lava splash, butterfingers.
import { ItemStack } from "@minecraft/server";
import { rand, spot, later } from "./helpers.js";

export const thunderMob = (id) => (ctx) => {
  const where = spot(ctx, 1);
  ctx.dimension.spawnEntity(id, where);
  later(5, () => ctx.dimension.spawnEntity("minecraft:lightning_bolt", where));
};

const BABY_KINDS = ["minecraft:cow", "minecraft:pig", "minecraft:sheep", "minecraft:chicken"];

export const babyShower = (ctx) => {
  for (let i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
    ctx.dimension.spawnEntity(`${BABY_KINDS[rand(BABY_KINDS.length)]}<minecraft:entity_born>`, spot(ctx, 1));
  }
};

export const butterfingers = (ctx) => {
  const inv = ctx.player.getComponent("minecraft:inventory")?.container;
  const slot = ctx.player.selectedSlotIndex;
  const item = inv?.getItem(slot);
  if (!item) throw new Error("empty hand");
  inv.setItem(slot, undefined);
  ctx.dimension.spawnItem(item, ctx.top);
};

export const lavaSplash = (ctx) => {
  const block = ctx.dimension.getBlock({ x: ctx.loc.x, y: ctx.loc.y + 3, z: ctx.loc.z });
  if (!block) throw new Error("block not loaded");
  block.setType("minecraft:lava");
  later(80, () => {
    if (block.typeId === "minecraft:lava") block.setType("minecraft:air");
  });
};

let anvilBusy = false;

export const fallingAnvil = (ctx) => {
  if (anvilBusy) throw new Error("an anvil is already falling");
  anvilBusy = true;
  const warn = (text) => {
    ctx.player.onScreenDisplay.setActionBar(text);
    ctx.player.playSound("note.bass");
  };
  later(20, () => warn("§c§lLook up! Something heavy is coming..."));
  later(40, () => warn("§4§lMOVE!"));
  later(60, () => {
    const at = ctx.player.location;
    const x = Math.floor(at.x);
    const z = Math.floor(at.z);
    const top = Math.floor(at.y) + 5;
    const block = ctx.dimension.getBlock({ x, y: top, z });
    if (!block) {
      anvilBusy = false;
      return;
    }
    block.setType("minecraft:anvil");
    later(20, () => {
      if (block.typeId === "minecraft:anvil") block.setType("minecraft:air");
    });
    later(70, () => {
      for (let y = top; y >= top - 8; y--) {
        const cell = ctx.dimension.getBlock({ x, y, z });
        if (cell && cell.typeId === "minecraft:anvil") cell.setType("minecraft:air");
      }
      anvilBusy = false;
    });
  });
  later(200, () => {
    anvilBusy = false;
  });
};

const CHEST_LOOT = [
  ["minecraft:iron_ingot", 6],
  ["minecraft:gold_ingot", 4],
  ["minecraft:diamond", 2],
  ["minecraft:emerald", 4],
  ["minecraft:golden_apple", 1],
  ["minecraft:cooked_beef", 8],
  ["minecraft:ender_pearl", 2],
  ["minecraft:bread", 8],
  ["minecraft:coal", 12],
  ["minecraft:obsidian", 4],
  ["minecraft:lapis_lazuli", 8],
  ["minecraft:redstone", 12],
];

export const lightningChest = (ctx) => {
  const player = ctx.player.location;
  const px = Math.floor(player.x);
  const pz = Math.floor(player.z);
  const y = ctx.loc.y + 1;
  const spots = [];
  for (let dx = -1; dx <= 1; dx++) {
    for (let dz = -1; dz <= 1; dz++) {
      if (dx === 0 && dz === 0) continue;
      const x = ctx.loc.x + dx;
      const z = ctx.loc.z + dz;
      if (x === px && z === pz) continue;
      const cell = ctx.dimension.getBlock({ x, y, z });
      if (!cell || !cell.isAir) continue;
      const next = [[1, 0], [-1, 0], [0, 1], [0, -1]].some(([ox, oz]) => {
        const near = ctx.dimension.getBlock({ x: x + ox, y, z: z + oz });
        return near && near.typeId === "minecraft:chest";
      });
      if (!next) spots.push({ x, z });
    }
  }
  if (spots.length === 0) throw new Error("no free spot");
  const at = spots[rand(spots.length)];
  const loot = [];
  const pool = CHEST_LOOT.slice();
  for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) loot.push(pool.splice(rand(pool.length), 1)[0]);
  const place = (height) => {
    const block = ctx.dimension.getBlock({ x: at.x, y: height, z: at.z });
    if (!block) throw new Error("block not loaded");
    block.setType("minecraft:chest");
    const container = block.getComponent("minecraft:inventory")?.container;
    if (!container) throw new Error("chest inventory missing");
    loot.forEach(([id, n], slot) => container.setItem(slot, new ItemStack(id, n)));
  };
  const remove = (height) => {
    const block = ctx.dimension.getBlock({ x: at.x, y: height, z: at.z });
    if (block) block.setType("minecraft:air");
  };
  let height = y + 8;
  place(height);
  const fall = () => {
    remove(height);
    height -= 1;
    place(height);
    if (height > y) {
      later(2, fall);
      return;
    }
    for (let s = 0; s < 5; s++) {
      later(s * 20 + 1, () => {
        ctx.player.onScreenDisplay.setActionBar(`§eLoot chest! Lightning in ${5 - s}...`);
        ctx.player.playSound("note.bass");
      });
    }
    later(100, () => {
      const target = { x: at.x + 0.5, y, z: at.z + 0.5 };
      ctx.dimension.spawnEntity("minecraft:lightning_bolt", target);
      const block = ctx.dimension.getBlock({ x: at.x, y, z: at.z });
      if (!block || block.typeId !== "minecraft:chest") return;
      const container = block.getComponent("minecraft:inventory")?.container;
      if (container) {
        for (let slot = 0; slot < 27; slot++) {
          const item = container.getItem(slot);
          if (item) ctx.dimension.spawnItem(item, target);
        }
      }
      block.setType("minecraft:air");
    });
  };
  later(2, fall);
};
```

## FILE: MCPEDL/Maps/One-Block-Skyblock-Lucky-Blocks/behavior_pack/scripts/lib/helpers.js (47 lines, 1687 bytes)

```
// Small building blocks used by prizes: random picks, item and mob spawns, effects, delayed tasks.
import { system, ItemStack } from "@minecraft/server";

export const rand = (n) => Math.floor(Math.random() * n);
export const spot = (ctx, lift) => ({ x: ctx.top.x + rand(3) - 1, y: ctx.loc.y + lift, z: ctx.top.z + rand(3) - 1 });

export const items = (list) => (ctx) => {
  for (const [id, n] of list) ctx.dimension.spawnItem(new ItemStack(id, n), ctx.top);
};
export const kit = (...ids) => items(ids.map((id) => [id, 1]));
export const mobs = (id, n, lift = 1) => (ctx) => {
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) ctx.dimension.spawnEntity(id, spot(ctx, lift));
};
export const effect = (id, seconds, amplifier = 0) => (ctx) => {
  ctx.player.addEffect(id, seconds * 20, { amplifier });
};
export const levels = (n) => (ctx) => {
  ctx.player.addLevels(n);
};
export const tax = (n) => (ctx) => {
  ctx.player.addLevels(-Math.min(n, ctx.player.level));
};
export const hurt = (n) => (ctx) => {
  ctx.player.applyDamage(n);
};
export const both = (...runs) => (ctx) => {
  for (const run of runs) run(ctx);
};
export const later = (ticks, fn) => {
  system.runTimeout(() => {
    try {
      fn();
    } catch (e) {
      return;
    }
  }, ticks);
};
export const strikes = (n) => (ctx) => {
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) later(i * 15 + 1, () => ctx.dimension.spawnEntity("minecraft:lightning_bolt", ctx.top));
};
export const booms = (n, radius) => (ctx) => {
  for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) later(i * 20 + 1, () => ctx.dimension.createExplosion(ctx.top, radius, { breaksBlocks: false, causesFire: false }));
};
export const fire = (seconds) => (ctx) => {
  ctx.player.setOnFire(seconds);
};
```

## FILE: MCPEDL/Maps/One-Block-Skyblock-Lucky-Blocks/behavior_pack/scripts/lib/island.js (75 lines, 2564 bytes)

```
// First join: builds the island, starter chest and spawn point, tags the player, and shows the first tip.
import { world, system, ItemStack } from "@minecraft/server";
import { LUCKY, TIP_KEY, BUILT_KEY, SETUP_TAG, CORE, SPAWN_BLOCK, SPAWN_STAND, CHEST_AT, STARTER } from "./config.js";

let setupRunning = false;

function blockAt(dimension, x, y, z) {
  const block = dimension.getBlock({ x, y, z });
  if (!block) throw new Error("chunk not loaded");
  return block;
}

function buildIsland() {
  const dimension = world.getDimension("overworld");
  for (let x = -16; x <= 16; x++) {
    for (let z = -16; z <= 16; z++) blockAt(dimension, x, -64, z).setType("minecraft:air");
  }
  blockAt(dimension, 0, 63, 0).setType("minecraft:bedrock");
  for (let x = -1; x <= 1; x++) {
    for (let z = -1; z <= 1; z++) blockAt(dimension, x, 64, z).setType("minecraft:grass_block");
  }
  blockAt(dimension, CORE.x, CORE.y, CORE.z).setType(LUCKY);
  const chest = blockAt(dimension, CHEST_AT.x, CHEST_AT.y, CHEST_AT.z);
  chest.setType("minecraft:chest");
  const container = chest.getComponent("minecraft:inventory")?.container;
  if (!container) throw new Error("chest inventory missing");
  STARTER.forEach(([id, amount], slot) => container.setItem(slot, new ItemStack(id, amount)));
  world.setDefaultSpawnLocation(SPAWN_BLOCK);
  world.setDynamicProperty(BUILT_KEY, true);
}

function runSetup(player, tries) {
  try {
    buildIsland();
    player.teleport(SPAWN_STAND);
    player.addTag(SETUP_TAG);
    setupRunning = false;
  } catch (e) {
    if (tries >= 15) {
      setupRunning = false;
      return;
    }
    system.runTimeout(() => runSetup(player, tries + 1), 20);
  }
}

export function registerIsland() {
  world.afterEvents.playerSpawn.subscribe(({ player, initialSpawn }) => {
    if (!initialSpawn || world.getDynamicProperty(TIP_KEY)) return;
    world.setDynamicProperty(TIP_KEY, true);
    system.runTimeout(() => {
      try {
        player.onScreenDisplay.setActionBar("§6Break the lucky block under you. Open the chest first. The first 20 breaks are a safe tutorial.");
      } catch (e) {
        return;
      }
    }, 100);
  });

  world.afterEvents.playerSpawn.subscribe(({ player, initialSpawn }) => {
    if (!initialSpawn || setupRunning) return;
    try {
      if (player.hasTag(SETUP_TAG)) return;
      if (world.getDynamicProperty(BUILT_KEY)) {
        player.addTag(SETUP_TAG);
        return;
      }
    } catch (e) {
      return;
    }
    setupRunning = true;
    system.runTimeout(() => runSetup(player, 0), 20);
  });
}
```

## FILE: MCPEDL/Maps/One-Block-Skyblock-Lucky-Blocks/behavior_pack/scripts/lib/Oneblock_Progression.js (1173 lines, 50865 bytes)

```
/**
 * Oneblock_Progression.js
 * Pure Data Pool Foundation for OneBlock Maps, Add-ons, and Minigames.
 * Target: Minecraft Bedrock 1.26.50 (all ids are Bedrock ids, not Java ids).
 *
 * ARCHITECTURE PRINCIPLE:
 * - This file ONLY holds the DATA POOLS (Blocks, Chest Loot, Mobs, Phase Milestones & Themes).
 * - Pack-specific gameplay logic, spawn mechanics, triggers, chest roll rates, custom events,
 *   cooldowns, and island systems belong inside the individual pack's scripts.
 * - Packs import or read from this progression pool without mutating the shared definitions.
 *
 * PROGRESSION ORDER (classic OneBlock):
 *   Overworld biomes -> Caves & Deep Dark -> Nether -> The End.
 *   1 Plains, 2 Underground, 3 Snowy Taiga, 4 Ocean, 5 Jungle, 6 Desert, 7 Badlands,
 *   8 Savanna Plains, 9 Dark Forest & Mushroom Fields, 10 Mangrove Swamp, 11 Cherry Grove,
 *   12 Pale Garden, 13 Windswept Highlands, 14 Frozen Peaks & Ice Spikes, 15 Craftsman's Workshop,
 *   16 Lush & Dripstone Caves, 17 Deepslate Depths, 18 Trial Chambers, 19 Deep Dark,
 *   20 Nether Wastes, 21 Crimson & Warped Forests, 22 Bastion & Basalt Deltas,
 *   23 The End, 24 End City & Void Horizon (final, endless).
 *
 * UNLOCK MODEL:
 * - Each phase lists only what it UNLOCKS. Earlier content is never removed.
 * - OneblockPoolProvider.sampleBlock/sampleChestItem/sampleMob(phase) sample from every
 *   phase up to the current one. The newest phase's share of the weight ramps from 40%
 *   (phase 2) to 60% (last phase); older phases share the rest evenly.
 * - Pass { exclusive: true } to sample the current phase only, or { currentShare: 0.5 } to fix the share.
 * - The last phase keeps unlocking nothing new: the full pool stays active forever.
 *
 * POOL NOTES:
 * - Block pools hold full solid blocks only (no plants or fluids), so they survive on the OneBlock.
 * - Chest pools hold no buckets, fluids, powders, spawn eggs, spawners or shulker items.
 * - Mob entries may carry tags: "aquatic" (needs water) and "boss" (very strong). sampleMob skips
 *   both by default; pass { excludeTags: [] } to allow them, or your own list to filter.
 * - `blocksRequired` is per phase (not cumulative). The last phase never ends.
 */

/**
 * @typedef {Object} BlockPoolEntry
 * @property {string} id - Minecraft block identifier (e.g. "minecraft:grass_block")
 * @property {number} weight - Relative probability weight in the pool
 * @property {Record<string, any>} [states] - Optional block state permutations
 */

/**
 * @typedef {Object} ChestPoolEntry
 * @property {string} id - Minecraft item identifier (e.g. "minecraft:oak_sapling")
 * @property {number} min - Minimum stack count
 * @property {number} max - Maximum stack count
 * @property {number} weight - Relative weight in the pool
 */

/**
 * @typedef {Object} MobPoolEntry
 * @property {string} id - Entity identifier (e.g. "minecraft:cow")
 * @property {number} weight - Relative spawn weight in the pool
 * @property {string[]} [tags] - Optional tags: "aquatic" (needs water), "boss" (very strong)
 */

/**
 * @typedef {Object} PhaseDataPool
 * @property {number} phase - Phase index (1-based)
 * @property {string} name - Phase title
 * @property {string} description - Lore / theme description
 * @property {number} blocksRequired - Base block break milestone to finish phase
 * @property {string} bedrockReplacement - Safe fallback block when breaking
 * @property {BlockPoolEntry[]} blocks - Block pool
 * @property {ChestPoolEntry[]} chests - Chest item loot pool
 * @property {MobPoolEntry[]} mobs - Entity spawn pool
 */

/**
 * Foundation Phase Pools
 * @type {PhaseDataPool[]}
 */

// Alias for backward compatibility
export const ONEBLOCK_PROGRESSION = ONEBLOCK_PROGRESSION_POOLS;

/** Unlock mode: share of total weight given to the newest phase. Ramps from START (phase 2) to END (last phase). */
const CURRENT_SHARE_START = 0.4;
const CURRENT_SHARE_END = 0.6;

/** Mob tags skipped by default when sampling mobs. Pass { excludeTags: [] } to allow everything. */
const DEFAULT_MOB_EXCLUDE_TAGS = ["aquatic", "boss"];

/**
 * Pure Data Pool Accessor
 * Only provides sampling and lookup against the pools.
 * Pack rules (spawn rates, mechanics) are implemented by the pack.
 */
export class OneblockPoolProvider {
  /**
   * @param {PhaseDataPool[]} [pools]
   */
  constructor(pools = ONEBLOCK_PROGRESSION_POOLS) {
    this.pools = pools;
    this._unlockCache = new Map();
  }

  /**
   * Get phase data pool by 1-based number
   * @param {number} phaseNumber
   * @returns {PhaseDataPool}
   */
  getPhasePool(phaseNumber) {
    const clampedIndex = Math.max(1, Math.min(phaseNumber, this.pools.length)) - 1;
    return this.pools[clampedIndex];
  }

  /**
   * Calculate milestone phase from broken block count
   * @param {number} totalBreaks
   * @returns {{ phase: PhaseDataPool, phaseIndex: number, phaseBreaks: number, isLastPhase: boolean }}
   */
  getPhaseFromBreaks(totalBreaks) {
    let accumulated = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < this.pools.length; i++) {
      const p = this.pools[i];
      if (totalBreaks < accumulated + p.blocksRequired) {
        return {
          phase: p,
          phaseIndex: i + 1,
          phaseBreaks: totalBreaks - accumulated,
          isLastPhase: false
        };
      }
      accumulated += p.blocksRequired;
    }
    const last = this.pools[this.pools.length - 1];
    return {
      phase: last,
      phaseIndex: this.pools.length,
      phaseBreaks: totalBreaks - accumulated,
      isLastPhase: true
    };
  }

  /**
   * Pick random item from a weighted pool
   * @template {{ weight: number }} T
   * @param {T[]} pool
   * @returns {T}
   */
  static sampleWeighted(pool) {
    const totalWeight = pool.reduce((sum, item) => sum + item.weight, 0);
    let rand = Math.random() * totalWeight;
    for (const item of pool) {
      if (rand < item.weight) return item;
      rand -= item.weight;
    }
    return pool[pool.length - 1];
  }

  /**
   * Cumulative "unlock" pool: everything introduced in phases 1..phaseNumber.
   * The current phase gets `currentShare` of the total weight so new content
   * feels fresh; the remainder is split evenly across earlier phases.
   * Duplicate ids are merged. Returns new objects; shared pools are never mutated.
   * @param {number} phaseNumber - 1-based phase (clamped; also valid past the last phase)
   * @param {"blocks"|"chests"|"mobs"} kind
   * @param {number} [currentShare] - 0..1 share for the current phase
   * @returns {Array<{ id: string, weight: number, min?: number, max?: number }>}
   */
  getUnlockedPool(phaseNumber, kind, currentShare) {
    const upto = Math.max(1, Math.min(Math.floor(phaseNumber) || 1, this.pools.length));
    if (currentShare === undefined) {
      const t = (upto - 1) / Math.max(1, this.pools.length - 1);
      currentShare = CURRENT_SHARE_START + (CURRENT_SHARE_END - CURRENT_SHARE_START) * t;
    }
    const key = `${kind}:${upto}:${currentShare}`;
    const cached = this._unlockCache.get(key);
    if (cached) return cached;

    const merged = new Map();
    for (let i = 0; i < upto; i++) {
      const list = this.pools[i][kind];
      const sum = list.reduce((s, e) => s + e.weight, 0);
      const share = upto === 1 ? 1 : (i === upto - 1 ? currentShare : (1 - currentShare) / (upto - 1));
      for (const e of list) {
        const w = (e.weight / sum) * share * 100;
        const prev = merged.get(e.id);
        if (!prev) {
          merged.set(e.id, { ...e, weight: w });
        } else {
          prev.weight += w;
          if (kind === "chests") {
            prev.min = Math.min(prev.min, e.min);
            prev.max = Math.max(prev.max, e.max);
          }
        }
      }
    }
    const out = [...merged.values()];
    this._unlockCache.set(key, out);
    return out;
  }

  /**
   * Resolve the pool to sample from.
   * Default = unlock mode (earlier phases stay available). `exclusive: true` = this phase only.
   */
  _resolvePool(phaseNumber, kind, options = {}) {
    const pool = options.exclusive
      ? this.getPhasePool(phaseNumber)[kind]
      : this.getUnlockedPool(phaseNumber, kind, options.currentShare);
    if (kind !== "mobs") return pool;
    const skip = options.excludeTags ?? DEFAULT_MOB_EXCLUDE_TAGS;
    if (!skip.length) return pool;
    const allowed = pool.filter((m) => !(m.tags ?? []).some((t) => skip.includes(t)));
    return allowed.length ? allowed : pool;
  }

  /** Sample a block (unlock mode by default) */
  sampleBlock(phaseNumber, options) {
    return OneblockPoolProvider.sampleWeighted(this._resolvePool(phaseNumber, "blocks", options));
  }

  /** Sample a chest item (unlock mode by default) */
  sampleChestItem(phaseNumber, options) {
    return OneblockPoolProvider.sampleWeighted(this._resolvePool(phaseNumber, "chests", options));
  }

  /** Sample a mob (unlock mode by default) */
  sampleMob(phaseNumber, options) {
    return OneblockPoolProvider.sampleWeighted(this._resolvePool(phaseNumber, "mobs", options));
  }
}

// Backward-compatible alias
export const OneBlockProgressionManager = OneblockPoolProvider;
```
