```
// The 20-break tutorial: fixed safe rewards given in random order, tracked in a saved world value.
import { world } from "@minecraft/server";
import { TUTORIAL_KEY } from "./config.js";
import { rand, items, kit, mobs, both, later } from "./helpers.js";

export const TUTORIAL = [
  ["Oak Logs", items([["minecraft:oak_log", 12]]), "Craft logs into planks in your inventory"],
  ["Oak Planks", items([["minecraft:oak_planks", 24]]), "Planks make tables, tools and blocks"],
  ["Sticks", items([["minecraft:stick", 16]]), "Sticks are part of every tool"],
  ["Crafting Table", items([["minecraft:crafting_table", 1]]), "Place it to craft bigger recipes"],
  ["Wooden Pickaxe and Axe", kit("minecraft:wooden_pickaxe", "minecraft:wooden_axe"), "Pickaxe for stone, axe for wood"],
  ["Wooden Shovel and Sword", kit("minecraft:wooden_shovel", "minecraft:wooden_sword"), "A sword keeps early mobs away"],
  ["Leather Helmet and Chestplate", kit("minecraft:leather_helmet", "minecraft:leather_chestplate"), "Equip armor from your inventory"],
  ["Leather Leggings and Boots", kit("minecraft:leather_leggings", "minecraft:leather_boots"), "Wear all four pieces for full protection"],
  ["Dirt", items([["minecraft:dirt", 32]]), "Dirt helps you grow your island"],
  ["Cobblestone", items([["minecraft:cobblestone", 32]]), "Your first strong building block"],
  ["Sand", items([["minecraft:sand", 16]]), "Smelt sand in a furnace to make glass"],
  ["Furnace and Coal", items([["minecraft:furnace", 1], ["minecraft:coal", 8]]), "Furnaces cook food and smelt ores"],
  ["Torches", items([["minecraft:torch", 16]]), "Light keeps mobs from spawning"],
  ["Food Basket", items([["minecraft:bread", 8], ["minecraft:cooked_beef", 6]]), "Eat when your hunger bar drops"],
  ["Crop Seeds", items([["minecraft:wheat_seeds", 8], ["minecraft:carrot", 4], ["minecraft:potato", 4]]), "Plant seeds on dirt near water"],
  ["Saplings and Bone Meal", items([["minecraft:oak_sapling", 4], ["minecraft:bone_meal", 8]]), "Plant saplings on grass, then use bone meal"],
  ["Water Bucket", items([["minecraft:water_bucket", 1]]), "Water makes farmland and an endless supply"],
  ["Bed", items([["minecraft:red_bed", 1]]), "Sleep to skip the night and set your spawn"],
  ["Farm Animals", both(mobs("minecraft:cow", 1), mobs("minecraft:sheep", 1)), "Animals give food and wool"],
  ["Birch Logs", items([["minecraft:birch_log", 12]]), "Mix wood types for different looks"],
];

const tutorialRaw = () => world.getDynamicProperty(TUTORIAL_KEY);
export const tutorialActive = () => tutorialRaw() !== "done";

export function takeTutorial() {
  const raw = tutorialRaw();
  if (raw === "done") return null;
  const left = typeof raw === "string" && raw.length > 0 ? raw.split(",").map(Number) : TUTORIAL.map((entry, index) => index);
  const index = left.splice(rand(left.length), 1)[0];
  world.setDynamicProperty(TUTORIAL_KEY, left.length > 0 ? left.join(",") : "done");
  return { entry: TUTORIAL[index], number: TUTORIAL.length - left.length, last: left.length === 0 };
}

export function showTutorial(player, dimension, loc, step) {
  const ctx = { player, dimension, loc, top: { x: loc.x + 0.5, y: loc.y + 1.2, z: loc.z + 0.5 } };
  try {
    step.entry[1](ctx);
  } catch (e) {
    return;
  }
  try {
    player.onScreenDisplay.setActionBar(`§bTutorial ${step.number}/${TUTORIAL.length} §8|§r §a${step.entry[0]} §8-§r §7${step.entry[2]}`);
    player.playSound("random.levelup");
    dimension.spawnParticle("minecraft:villager_happy", ctx.top);
  } catch (e) {
    return;
  }
  if (step.last) later(80, () => player.onScreenDisplay.setActionBar("§6Tutorial complete! From now on every break is a random prize."));
}
```

## FILE: MCPEDL/Maps/One-Block-Skyblock-Lucky-Blocks/behavior_pack/scripts/lib/types.js (15 lines, 467 bytes)

```
// Picks the next lucky block type for the center spot, using each type's weight.
import { LUCKY, TYPES } from "./config.js";
import { tutorialActive } from "./tutorial.js";

export function nextBlockId() {
  if (tutorialActive()) return LUCKY;
  const ids = Object.keys(TYPES);
  let roll = Math.random() * ids.reduce((sum, id) => sum + TYPES[id].weight, 0);
  for (const id of ids) {
    roll -= TYPES[id].weight;
    if (roll < 0) return id;
  }
  return LUCKY;
}
```

## FILE: MCPEDL/Maps/One-Block-Skyblock-Lucky-Blocks/behavior_pack/scripts/main.js (65 lines, 2330 bytes)

```
// Entry point: wires the break event, the lucky block safety timer and the island setup together.
import { world, system } from "@minecraft/server";
import { TYPES, CORE, SETUP_TAG } from "./lib/config.js";
import { rand } from "./lib/helpers.js";
import { PRIZES } from "./lib/prizes.js";
import { takeTutorial, showTutorial } from "./lib/tutorial.js";
import { nextBlockId } from "./lib/types.js";
import { registerIsland } from "./lib/island.js";

function announce(ctx, prize) {
  const good = prize[1] === "g";
  const line = `${ctx.type.label}§r §7${ctx.type.chance}% §8|§r ${good ? "§a" : "§c"}${prize[0]}`;
  try {
    ctx.player.onScreenDisplay.setActionBar(line);
    ctx.player.playSound(good ? "random.levelup" : "note.bass");
    ctx.dimension.spawnParticle(good ? "minecraft:villager_happy" : "minecraft:villager_angry", ctx.top);
  } catch (e) {
    return;
  }
}

function trigger(player, dimension, loc, type) {
  const ctx = { player, dimension, loc, type, top: { x: loc.x + 0.5, y: loc.y + 1.2, z: loc.z + 0.5 } };
  const pool = type.theme ? PRIZES.filter((p) => p[2].includes(type.theme)) : PRIZES;
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < 6; attempt++) {
    const prize = pool[rand(pool.length)];
    try {
      prize[3](ctx);
    } catch (e) {
      continue;
    }
    announce(ctx, prize);
    return;
  }
}

world.afterEvents.playerBreakBlock.subscribe((event) => {
  const type = TYPES[event.brokenBlockPermutation.type.id];
  if (!type) return;
  const { player, dimension, block } = event;
  const loc = { x: block.location.x, y: block.location.y, z: block.location.z };
  const isCore = dimension.id === "minecraft:overworld" && loc.x === CORE.x && loc.y === CORE.y && loc.z === CORE.z;
  system.run(() => {
    const step = isCore ? takeTutorial() : null;
    if (isCore) {
      const target = dimension.getBlock(loc);
      if (target && target.isAir) target.setType(nextBlockId());
    }
    if (step) showTutorial(player, dimension, loc, step);
    else trigger(player, dimension, loc, type);
  });
});

system.runInterval(() => {
  if (world.getPlayers({ tags: [SETUP_TAG] }).length === 0) return;
  try {
    const block = world.getDimension("overworld").getBlock(CORE);
    if (block && block.isAir) block.setType(nextBlockId());
  } catch (e) {
    return;
  }
}, 100);

registerIsland();
```

## FILE: MCPEDL/Maps/One-Block-Skyblock-Lucky-Blocks/CHANGELOG.md (93 lines, 5677 bytes)

```
# Changelog — One Block Skyblock Lucky Blocks

---

## [9.0.0] - 2026-11-23 - Starter Chest Fix

### Fixed
- **Starter chest**: The island function now fills the starter chest with the command Bedrock accepts, so the function loads without errors.

## [v8] - 2026-11-22 - Baby Shower and Split Scripts

### Added
- **Baby Shower**: About 10 baby cows, pigs, sheep and chickens spawn around the island.
- **49 more prizes**: The pool grows to 250 prizes, so each prize now has a 0.4% chance in the All-Round block.
### Improved & Balanced
- **Block chance on the action bar**: Each result now shows the chance of that block type.
- **One anvil only**: Falling Anvil drops a single anvil and removes it after it lands.
- **Split scripts**: The script is now split into modules in scripts/lib so it is easier to maintain.

## [v7] - 2026-11-22 - 20-Break Tutorial

### Added
- **20-break tutorial**: The first 20 breaks of the center block give 20 fixed, safe rewards in random order, such as wood, wooden tools, leather armor and building blocks, with the break number and a tip on the action bar.
### Improved & Balanced
- **Building is the most common type**: Building now appears 20% of the time, the highest of all types. All-Round is 17%, Farming, Combat and Mining 14% each, Mystic 12% and Treasure 9%.
- **Tutorial block**: The center block is always All-Round during the tutorial.

## [v6] - 2026-11-22 - 200 Equal Prizes

### Added
- **200 equal prizes**: Every break now picks from a flat list of 200 prizes. In the All-Round block each prize has the same 0.5% chance, and each themed block picks equally from its own list.
- **Falling Chest**: A loot chest falls next to the island and lightning strikes it 5 seconds after it lands.
- **New prizes**: 40 more prizes across farming, building, combat, mining, mystic and treasure, plus a few small setbacks.
### Improved & Balanced
- **Flat odds**: Luck tiers, the 70/30 split and the step-by-step progression are gone, and the heaviest setbacks (large hordes, wardens and chain explosions) were removed.
- **Luck Ledger removed**: The Luck Ledger item, its counters and its starter chest slot are gone.

## [v5] - 2026-11-21 - Lucky Block Types

### Added
- **Lucky block types**: The center block now comes back as one of 7 types: All-Round, Farming, Building, Combat, Mining, Mystic or Treasure. Each has its own texture and favors matching good results.
- **14 new results**: Wooden Sword, Wooden Pickaxe, Experience Spark, Archer Starter, Coal Starter, Book Stack, Emerald Find, Stone Sword, Stone Pickaxe, Lapis Pinch, Lucky Coin, Iron Blade, Arrow Quiver and Spare Shield.
- **Falling Anvil**: A warning shows on the action bar, then an anvil drops from above you.
- **Thunder Pig and Thunder Villager**: A mob appears and lightning strikes it about a quarter of a second later.
### Improved & Balanced
- **Action bar only**: Results, the first-join tip and the Luck Ledger now show on the action bar instead of in chat.

## [v4] - 2026-11-21 - Island Setup Fix

### Improved & Balanced
- **Spawn point**: You now start standing on the lucky block in the center of the island, and the world spawn is set there.
- **Island setup on first join**: The island, lucky block, starter chest and spawn point are now built when you first join, and the empty-world platform below is cleared.

## [v3] - 2026-11-21 - Lucky Block Textures

### Added
- **Lucky block look**: The lucky block now has its own gold texture, with a question mark on the sides, a four-leaf clover on top and a plain gold bottom.

## [v2] - 2026-11-10

### Added
- **Starter chest**: A chest on the island corner holds 3 Oak Saplings, 4 Bone Meal, 4 Bread, a Bucket and a Luck Ledger.
- **Luck Ledger**: Use it to see your break count and how many Four-Leaf Fortune or Jinx breaks are left.
- **First-join tip**: A short chat message tells new players to break the lucky block and open the chest.

### Improved & Balanced
- **Building materials first**: Early Common and Uncommon results now lean toward saplings, logs, planks, dirt, seeds and stone, with 8 more good results in total.



### Added
- **Silent progression**: Results now follow a natural playthrough from Plains to the End and beyond, moving up one step every 30 breaks without any announcement.
- **20 tiers per side**: Twenty good tiers and twenty bad tiers, with 112 good results and 81 bad results, each tier with its own results.

### Improved & Balanced
- **70/30 luck**: Every break now has a 70% chance of good luck and a 30% chance of bad luck.
- **Tier odds**: The current tier comes up 50% of the time, with the next tiers up at 25%, 12.5%, 6.25% and 6.25%.
- **Luck changes**: Rabbit's Foot, Four-Leaf Fortune, Golden Horseshoe and Jinx now work on the 70/30 odds.
- **No guaranteed streak**: The first 30 breaks are no longer always good luck.



### Added
- **Lucky block island**: A 3x3 grass island in the void with one central lucky block that returns after every break.
- **30 guaranteed good breaks**: The first 30 breaks always give good luck, followed by an even split between good and bad luck.
- **Luck tiers and 76 results**: Common, Rare and Epic good luck, and Mischief, Bad Luck and Cursed bad luck, each shown with a message, sound and particles.
- **Luck influence**: A Rabbit's Foot in the off hand and the Four-Leaf Fortune, Golden Horseshoe and Jinx results change the odds of the next breaks.

## [1.1.0] - 2026-11-10

### Improved & Balanced
- **Luck changes**: The Rabbit's Foot off-hand bonus is removed. Luck now changes only through the Four-Leaf Fortune, Golden Horseshoe and Jinx results, and the good luck chance stays between 50% and 90%.
```

## FILE: MCPEDL/Maps/One-Block-Skyblock-Lucky-Blocks/DESCRIPTION.md (97 lines, 6624 bytes)

```
# One Block Skyblock Lucky Blocks — A single lucky block on a 3x3 void island

**By ALNStudios**

[![Minecraft Bedrock](https://img.shields.io/badge/Minecraft%20Bedrock-Stable%20Release-brightgreen?style=flat-square&logo=minecraft)](https://github.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges)
[![Format](https://img.shields.io/badge/Format-.MCPACK%20%7C%20.MCADDON%20%7C%20.MCWORLD-blue?style=flat-square)](https://github.com/AllensCreations/MCPE-Curseforges)

**Break the one lucky block on a 3x3 void island and win one of 250 prizes, each with the same chance.**

> The center block comes back as one of 7 types, each with its own texture and a prize list that matches its purpose. Surprises such as a falling anvil or a loot chest struck by lightning keep every break exciting.

## Overview

You start on a 3x3 grass island floating in an empty void, standing on a single lucky block in the center. Break it and it returns in the same spot as a random type: All-Round, Farming, Building, Combat, Mining, Mystic or Treasure. Every break gives a prize: items, food, tools, friendly animals, potion effects, or a small setback such as a hostile mob or a short danger.

## Key Features

### One Central Lucky Block
- **What Happens**: The lucky block sits at the center of the island and is replaced the moment you break it, with a random type from the pool.
- **Good To Know**: If the center spot ever becomes empty, a lucky block is placed there again within about 5 seconds.

### 7 Lucky Block Types
- **What Happens**: The types are All-Round, Farming, Building, Combat, Mining, Mystic and Treasure. Each has its own texture with a symbol on the sides and top.
- **Good To Know**: A themed block gives prizes from its own list, such as saplings and food from Farming or swords and armor from Combat. The All-Round block can give any prize. After the tutorial, Building is the most common type at 20% of blocks and Treasure is the rarest at 9%.

### 20-Break Tutorial
- **What Happens**: The first 20 breaks of the center block give 20 fixed, safe rewards in random order: wood, planks, sticks, a crafting table, wooden tools, leather armor, building blocks, a furnace, torches, food, seeds, saplings, a water bucket, a bed and farm animals.
- **Good To Know**: Each tutorial break shows its number and a short tip on the action bar, and no setbacks happen. During the tutorial the center block is always the All-Round type.

### 250 Equal Prizes
- **What Happens**: In the All-Round block every one of the 250 prizes has the same 0.4% chance. A themed block picks equally among the prizes of its own list.
- **Good To Know**: 198 prizes are rewards and 52 are setbacks, so about 79% of All-Round breaks give a reward. The same prize can come twice in a row.

### Physical Surprises
- **What Happens**: Falling Anvil warns you on the action bar, then drops one anvil about 5 blocks above you, and it is removed a few seconds after it lands. Thunder Pig and Thunder Villager summon a mob and call lightning on it about a quarter of a second later.
- **Good To Know**: Lightning strikes close to the island, so it can hurt you and set you on fire.

### Baby Shower
- **What Happens**: About 10 baby cows, pigs, sheep and chickens spawn around the island.

### Falling Chest
- **What Happens**: A loot chest falls from about 8 blocks up onto a free spot next to the center block and holds 5 stacks of loot. Lightning strikes it 5 seconds after it lands.
- **Good To Know**: Anything still inside when the lightning hits is spilled on the ground at the chest spot.

### Starter Chest
- **What Happens**: A chest waits on a corner of the island with 3 Oak Saplings, 4 Bone Meal, 4 Bread and a Bucket.
- **Good To Know**: The first time you join, a short action bar tip tells you to break the lucky block and open the chest.

### Action Bar Results
- **What Happens**: Each prize shows on the action bar with the block type, its chance of appearing and the prize name, and nothing is written in chat. Tutorial breaks show their number and a tip instead.

## How to Use

1. Open the world and wait a moment while the island appears; you spawn standing on the lucky block in the center, so look down to break it.
2. Read the prize on the action bar, then pick up what dropped or deal with what appeared.
3. Open the chest on the corner for your first saplings and tools.
4. Follow the 20-break tutorial, then keep breaking the block that comes back and try each type.

## Common Questions

- **Does the lucky block run out?**: No. It is placed again each time it is broken, so you can keep going without limit.
- **Can I choose the type?**: No. The type is random each time the block comes back.
- **Do the prizes get better as I go?**: No. After the 20-break tutorial every break draws from the same list, so any prize can come at any time.

## Compatibility

- Minecraft Bedrock Edition: 1.26.60 (release 26.60)
- Experimental features: No experiment requirement detected in the inspected files.
- Achievements: Yes, achievement-friendly. World settings of the included worlds were not inspected, and using cheats disables achievements.
- Verification: Source created, not tested in game.
```
